source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=3366f4a4b80e5943bee2feea8764f04f1d0caa3a04ecc1b1a51faaeb621d7008</small><pre>foreach dtaset in small medium large {&#10;&#10;	// load trade data&#10;	use ${usr_data}/data_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, clear&#10;	&#10;	* preliminary variable definitions&#10;	rename RTA_DGD RTA&#10;	egen id_ci = group(exporter)&#10;	egen id_cj = group(importer)&#10;&#10;	* set panel IDs&#10;	xtset id_ci_cj year, yearly&#10;&#10;	* determine treatment cohorts&#10;	egen first_treat=csgvar(RTA), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;	* shift RTA/WTO 3 years forward for anticipation effects&#10;	gen RTA_3 = F3.RTA&#10;	egen first_treat_3=csgvar(RTA_3), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;	gen WTO_3 = F3.WTO&#10;	&#10;	* define exclude dummy for three anticipation years&#10;	gen exclude = (first_treat-1==year &#124; first_treat-2==year &#124; first_treat-3==year)&#10;	&#10;	// drop always-treated units&#10;	drop if first_treat_3 == 1980&#10;&#10;	// drop exits&#10;	sort exporter importer year&#10;	gen D_RTA = 0&#10;	replace D_RTA = RTA-RTA[_n-1] if id_ci_cj==id_ci_cj[_n-1]&#10;	egen exit = min(D_RTA), by(id_ci_cj)&#10;	drop if exit==-1&#10;	drop exit D_RTA&#10;&#10;	// define 5-year interval&#10;	gen int_5yr = mod(year,5)&#10;	&#10;	// define border-time ID&#10;	gen brdr=1 if exporter!=importer&#10;	replace brdr=0 if brdr==.&#10;	egen brdr_time=group(brdr year)&#10;	&#10;	// define variables&#10;	gen lnDIST = ln(DIST)&#10;	gen lntrade = ln(trade)&#10;&#10;	// save data set&#10;	save ${usr_data}/data_sdid_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, replace&#10;&#10;}&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>sample.trade_share_threshold.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:135-151; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=fba0a33bea533832ae9cb0f0e4b82c00c127b52036c1558586b3c406e4072f58</small><pre>use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;keep if exporter!=importer&#10;collapse (sum) trade, by(exporter)&#10;sort trade&#10;gen sum_trade_cum=sum(trade)&#10;egen sum_trade_all=sum(trade)&#10;gen ratio= sum_trade_cum/sum_trade_all&#10;drop if ratio&lt;0.01&#10;keep exporter&#10;save ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta, replace&#10;rename exporter importer&#10;save ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta, replace&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;joinby importer using ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta&#10;joinby exporter using ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta&#10;keep year exporter importer trade CLNY CNTG LANG DIST RTA_DGD WTO_DGD gdp_pwt_cur_d gdp_pwt_cur_o idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_medium.dta, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>sample.trade_share_threshold_small.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:153-171; Variable_Construction.do:1349-1363; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=820cb847059b1fb8cdc35e195d17e27547d3d0e21097bd408d17a9340c22fe0b; code_sha256=37143809ece890277031b5fbb08256103c657ae4724e42fe9dfe385a2532a41d</small><pre>** Clean miscellaneous household variables &#10;gen owns_house = 0&#10;replace owns_house = 1 if b_owns_house == 1&#10;&#10;gen elec_access = 0&#10;replace elec_access = 1 if b_elec_w_acc == 1&#10;&#10;replace a_hectares = 0 if a_hectares == .&#10;&#10;rename b_total_neighbors tot_neigh&#10;&#10;rename b_neighbor_has_cistern neigh_cist&#10;replace neigh_cist = 0 if neigh_cist == 2&#10;&#10;rename b_last_grade_hoh last_grade_hoh&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>integrity.zip_using_rows_matched.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/z15288849/distance_prep.do:145-157; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=0e31f436055b5d378ff5011e420d93a34ce296168409fbc7bb1017b2d0a04f65; code_sha256=086a6843d12e16fc64db0a1d3c9c0ee66e8b66e80655d1d5e7afefe2495d5c82</small><pre>use raw_data/zips_raw.dta&#10;&#10;* Merge w/ list of catchment-eligible MTFs from Part 1&#10;merge 1:1 ZIP using temp/catchment_dmisids_20`yr&#x27;.dta, gen(zipmerge)&#10;assert zipmerge != 2 // Ensuring that all of the MTFs were matched to a zip code&#10;keep if faczip != &quot;&quot;&#10;&#10;* Prepare data for joinby with master zip code list&#10;keep faccntry facstate faczip dmisnme dmisid instalnm faccity LAT LONG joinby&#10;rename LAT faclat&#10;rename LONG faclong&#10;&#10;save temp/catchment_dmisids_20`yr&#x27;.dta, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>panel.cohort_anticipation_exit_filter.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:177-227; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=3366f4a4b80e5943bee2feea8764f04f1d0caa3a04ecc1b1a51faaeb621d7008</small><pre>foreach dtaset in small medium large {&#10;&#10;	// load trade data&#10;	use ${usr_data}/data_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, clear&#10;	&#10;	* preliminary variable definitions&#10;	rename RTA_DGD RTA&#10;	egen id_ci = group(exporter)&#10;	egen id_cj = group(importer)&#10;&#10;	* set panel IDs&#10;	xtset id_ci_cj year, yearly&#10;&#10;	* determine treatment cohorts&#10;	egen first_treat=csgvar(RTA), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;	* shift RTA/WTO 3 years forward for anticipation effects&#10;	gen RTA_3 = F3.RTA&#10;	egen first_treat_3=csgvar(RTA_3), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;	gen WTO_3 = F3.WTO&#10;	&#10;	* define exclude dummy for three anticipation years&#10;	gen exclude = (first_treat-1==year &#124; first_treat-2==year &#124; first_treat-3==year)&#10;	&#10;	// drop always-treated units&#10;	drop if first_treat_3 == 1980&#10;&#10;	// drop exits&#10;	sort exporter importer year&#10;	gen D_RTA = 0&#10;	replace D_RTA = RTA-RTA[_n-1] if id_ci_cj==id_ci_cj[_n-1]&#10;	egen exit = min(D_RTA), by(id_ci_cj)&#10;	drop if exit==-1&#10;	drop exit D_RTA&#10;&#10;	// define 5-year interval&#10;	gen int_5yr = mod(year,5)&#10;	&#10;	// define border-time ID&#10;	gen brdr=1 if exporter!=importer&#10;	replace brdr=0 if brdr==.&#10;	egen brdr_time=group(brdr year)&#10;	&#10;	// define variables&#10;	gen lnDIST = ln(DIST)&#10;	gen lntrade = ln(trade)&#10;&#10;	// save data set&#10;	save ${usr_data}/data_sdid_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, replace&#10;&#10;}&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>robustness.dynamic_did_preperiod_placebo.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_recruitment_regressions.do:562-582; source_kind=deposited_research_code_with_community_command_context; source_sha256=8396153c08ca59b6cae0eb9285340b55bc6731cff9029a9fb8f29ed8dfcde048; code_sha256=a945df6e81239f1bc31be7809a34133262197670d7068537968cf908d8947218</small><pre>	use &quot;Data/clean/Base_ADPAnalysis.dta&quot;, clear&#10;	&#10;	* Placebo treatment	&#10;	rename PostChangePpal PostChangePpal_pre&#10;	rename t_pla t_pla_pre&#10;	rename MinYearPpalChange MinYearPpalChange_pre&#10;		&#10;	keep if t_pla_pre&gt;=-4 &amp; t_pla_pre &lt;= 3 &amp; year &gt;= 2009 &amp; year &lt;= 2012 &amp; Dmun == 1&#10;	drop min_adp	&#10;	g min_adp = year if t_pla_pre == 0&#10;	spread min_adp, by(rbd)&#10;	&#10;	keep SDall_fe_prin_2wayfe PostChangePpal_pre rbd year min_adp&#10;	&#10;	twowayfeweights SDall_fe_prin_2wayfe rbd year PostChangePpal_pre,  type(feTR)&#10;	di 192/555&#10;	* Sum of negative weights  -.01237145&#10;		&#10;	local w = 1&#10;	local w1 = 1&#10;	did_multiplegt SDall_fe_prin_2wayfe rbd year PostChangePpal_pre, placebo(`w1&#x27;) breps(20) robust_dynamic dynamic(`w&#x27;) cluster(rbd) covariances seed(1111001) average_effect&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>robustness.placebo_renamed_treatment.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_recruitment_regressions.do:75-86; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=8396153c08ca59b6cae0eb9285340b55bc6731cff9029a9fb8f29ed8dfcde048; code_sha256=527daddcf0a8680c304e4b15802b777ef0be3e6ae2acf677c9b91c9803f217ca</small><pre>	* Only Public: PLACEBO&#10;	rename PostChangePpal aux&#10;	rename PostChangePpal_pre PostChangePpal&#10;	reghdfe SDall_fe_prin_2wayfe PostChangePpal if t_pla_pre&gt;=-4 &amp; t_pla_pre &lt;= 3 &amp; year &gt;= 2009 &amp; year &lt;= 2012 &amp; Dmun == 1 , a(year rbd)  vce(cluster rbd)&#10;		distinct rbd if e(sample) == 1&#10;		local N = `r(ndistinct)&#x27;&#10;		sum `e(depvar)&#x27; if e(sample)&#10;		local mean = `r(mean)&#x27;&#10;		codebook rbd if e(sample) == 1 &amp; PostChangePpal == 1&#10;		$out `append&#x27; adds(# of Schools, `N&#x27;, Mean Dep Var, `mean&#x27;) addtext(Year FE, Yes, School FE, Yes, School controls, No, Municipality controls, No)&#10;	rename PostChangePpal PostChangePpal_pre &#10;	rename aux PostChangePpal&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>sample.trade_share_threshold.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:135-151; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=fba0a33bea533832ae9cb0f0e4b82c00c127b52036c1558586b3c406e4072f58</small><pre>use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;keep if exporter!=importer&#10;collapse (sum) trade, by(exporter)&#10;sort trade&#10;gen sum_trade_cum=sum(trade)&#10;egen sum_trade_all=sum(trade)&#10;gen ratio= sum_trade_cum/sum_trade_all&#10;drop if ratio&lt;0.01&#10;keep exporter&#10;save ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta, replace&#10;rename exporter importer&#10;save ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta, replace&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;joinby importer using ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta&#10;joinby exporter using ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta&#10;keep year exporter importer trade CLNY CNTG LANG DIST RTA_DGD WTO_DGD gdp_pwt_cur_d gdp_pwt_cur_o idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_medium.dta, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>sample.trade_share_threshold_small.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:153-171; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=65d25ded06a2515cfcf05e86f6cc3a5620e55443783cef69525dc5e18c68bd00</small><pre>*g. Create small sample*&#10;**************************&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;keep if exporter!=importer&#10;collapse (sum) trade, by(exporter)&#10;sort trade&#10;gen sum_trade_cum=sum(trade)&#10;egen sum_trade_all=sum(trade)&#10;gen ratio= sum_trade_cum/sum_trade_all&#10;drop if ratio&lt;0.02&#10;keep exporter&#10;save ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta, replace&#10;rename exporter importer&#10;save ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta, replace&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;joinby importer using ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta&#10;joinby exporter using ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta&#10;keep year exporter importer trade CLNY CNTG LANG DIST RTA_DGD WTO_DGD gdp_pwt_cur_d gdp_pwt_cur_o idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_small.dta, replace&#10;</pre></details> | <details><summary><code>dataset.finalize_section_adjacency_drop.v1 [exclude]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/173341/dofiles/3. c_voter_section_no_ == c_voter_section_no_[_n-1] &#10;&#10;&#10;*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*&#10;**## Final Treatment Variables &#10;*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*&#10;&#10;rename tot_treatment_by_section_1 tot_treat_by_section_1&#10;&#10;&#10;** Keep only variables used in final analysis&#10;keep c_mun_id mun_id c_voter_section_no_ location_id c_vote_here_ b_clusters ///&#10;name_match vp_match pty_match coalition_match eligible_in_2008 ///&#10;incumbent_votes_section challenger_votes_section turnout blank_null PT_votes pres_coalition_votes gov_coalition_votes right_leaning_votes ///&#10;tot_treat_by_section_2* tot_study_2* eligible tot_treat_by_section_1 tot_study_1 ///&#10;tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 tot_study_frequent_1 tot_study_infrequent_1 ///&#10;avg_age avg_school avg_gender missing_age missing_school missing_gender ///&#10;candidate_A_incumbent* candidate_B_incumbent* candidate_C_incumbent*&#10;&#10;&#10;** Variable labels&#10;label var c_voter_section_no_ &quot;Voter section number&quot;&#10;label var c_mun_id &quot;Municipal ID&quot;&#10;label var eligible &quot;No. of registered voters&quot;&#10;label var turnout &quot;Turnout&quot;&#10;label var eligible_in_2008 &quot;No. of registered voters in 2008&quot;&#10;&#10;foreach cand in &quot;A&quot; &quot;B&quot; &quot;C&quot; {&#10;	&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is incumbent mayor&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_vp &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is incumbent vice-mayor&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_pty &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is from incumbent party&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_coalition &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is from incumbent coalition&quot;&#10;}&#10;&#10;label var location_id &quot;Voting location ID&quot;&#10;label var incumbent_votes_section &quot;Votes for incumbent mayor&quot;&#10;label var challenger_votes_section &quot;Votes for challenger candidate&quot;&#10;label var blank_null &quot;Blank and null votes&quot;&#10;label var name_match &quot;Incumbent mayor was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var vp_match &quot;Incumbent vice-mayor was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var pty_match &quot;Candidate from incumbent party was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var coalition_match &quot;Candidate from incumbent coalition was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var avg_age &quot;Respondent age (mean)&quot;&#10;label var missing_age &quot;Missing respondent age (mean, section)&quot;&#10;label var avg_school &quot;Respondent years of education (mean)&quot;&#10;label var missing_school &quot;Missing respondent years of education (mean, section)&quot;&#10;label var avg_gender &quot;Respondent is female (mean)&quot;&#10;label var missing_gender &quot;Missing respondent gender (mean, section)&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;** Save final electoral dataset for the overall sample&#10;quietly compress&#10;save &quot;${final_data_folder}/voting_data.dta&quot;, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>figure.event_study_row_order_parmest.v1 [exclude]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_recruitment_figures.do:15-64; t &lt;= 3 &amp; year &gt;= 2009 &amp; year &lt;= 2016 &amp; sample == 1 , a(year rbd t) vce(cluster rbd)&#10;		test d_4_t = d_3_t = d_2_t = 0&#10;		preserve&#10;				estimate store coef &#10;							&#10;			* Create data set of reg coefficients and confidence intervals &#10;				parmest, norestore&#10;&#10;			* Keep parameters of interest&#10;				drop if _n &gt; 7		&#10;				rename t tstat&#10;&#10;			* Time variable from and since event&#10;	&#10;				g       t = _n - 5 if _n &lt;= 3&#10;				replace t = _n - 4 if _n &gt; 3								&#10;								&#10;			* Add coefficient in t == -1&#10;				local tot = 1 + _N&#10;				set obs `tot&#x27;&#10;				replace t = -1 if _n == `tot&#x27;&#10;				replace estimate = 0 if estimate == .&#10;				replace min95 = 0 if min95 == .&#10;				replace max95 = 0 if max95 == .&#10;				sort t&#10;							&#10;			* Figure&#10;				twoway (scatter estimate t, mc(navy)) (rcap min95 max95 t, lc(gs12) fc(gs12)), ///&#10;					yline(0, lc(black) ) xline(-0.5, lc(navy) lw(medium) ) ///&#10;					graphregion(color(white)) ///&#10;					ylabel(-0.05(0.05)0.15, format(%9.2f)) /// &#10;					xlabel(-4(2)3) xtitle(&quot;Years since ADP&quot;) ///					&#10;					graphregion(color(white)) /// &#10;					scale(1.3) ///&#10;					legend(off)&#10;&#10;			* Export 			&#10;			graph export &quot;Figures/Figure_4_A.pdf&quot;, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>merge.political_sample_inner_join.v1 [exclude]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/173341/dofiles/1. b_`var&#x27;&#10;}&#10;&#10;&#10;* Update household identifier&#10;rename b_num_domicilio_from_loc num_domicilio_from_loc&#10;destring num_domicilio_from_loc, replace&#10;&#10;&#10;* Merge in the Political Round 1 sample&#10;merge 1:m b_sbjnum using &quot;${gen_data_folder}/clean_indiv_V6_V16.dta&quot;&#10;drop if _merge != 3&#10;drop _merge&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>simulation.jackknife_nested_loops.v1 [exclude]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/30_Table_1f_TWFE_jackknife_large.do:24-105; {&#10;&#10;	preserve&#10;	&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	duplicates drop exporter, force&#10;	gen randn = runiform()&#10;	egen rank=rank(randn), unique&#10;	keep if rank&gt;=46&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_exporter`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	rename exporter importer&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_importer`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	&#10;	restore&#10;&#10;}&#10;	&#10;* initialise matrices&#10;mat B_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat N_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;mat index = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat N_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat SE = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat loCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat hiCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;* open data set&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_large, clear&#10;&#10;* main estimation&#10;qui ppmlhdfe trade RTA_3, absorb(idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj brdr_time) cluster(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;mat B_temp = e(b)&#10;local B_main = B_temp[1,1]	&#10;local N_main = e(N)&#10;&#10;* run 1000 jackknife iterations&#10;forval jiter=1(1)`N_jiter&#x27; {&#10;&#10;	di &quot;`jiter&#x27;&quot;&#10;		&#10;	* open data set&#10;	use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_large, clear	&#10;&#10;	forval p=1(1)4 {&#10;&#10;		preserve&#10;		&#10;		capture drop _merge_id_ci&#10;		qui merge m:1 exporter using &quot;${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_exporter`jiter&#x27;.dta&quot;&#10;		rename _merge _merge_id_ci&#10;		capture drop _merge_id_cj&#10;		qui merge m:1 importer using &quot;${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_importer`jiter&#x27;.dta&quot;&#10;		rename _merge _merge_id_cj&#10;		&#10;		if `p&#x27;==1 {&#10;			qui keep if _merge_id_ci==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==2 {&#10;			qui drop if _merge_id_ci==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==3 {&#10;			qui keep if _merge_id_cj==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==4 {&#10;			qui drop if _merge_id_cj==3&#10;		}&#10;		&#10;		qui ppmlhdfe trade RTA_3, absorb(idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj brdr_time) cluster(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;		mat B_temp = e(b)&#10;		mat B_jack[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = B_temp[1,1]	&#10;		mat N_jack[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = e(N)&#10;		mat index[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = (`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;&#10;		&#10;		restore&#10;			&#10;	}&#10;</pre></details> | — | support gap: none |

treatment == 1 &#10;egen tot_study_1 = total(tot_study_temp) if c_voter_section_no_ != ., by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_study_1 &quot;Respondents&quot;&#10;&#10;gen tot_control_by_section_1 = tot_study_1 - tot_treatment_by_section_1&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF TREATED UNITS WITH CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION	&#10;gen treat_frequent = treatment * frequent_interactor&#10;label var treat_frequent &quot;Treatment $\times$ Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;						&#10;gen treat_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 1 &amp; treatment == 1&#10;egen tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 = total(treat_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Treated Individuals with Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop treat_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF CTRL UNITS WITH CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION &#10;gen ctrl_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 1 &amp; treatment == 0&#10;egen tot_ctrl_talk_by_section_1 = total(ctrl_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_ctrl_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Number of Control People Interact Often with Politician&quot;&#10;&#10;gen tot_study_frequent_1 = tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 + tot_ctrl_talk_by_section_1&#10;label variable tot_study_frequent_1 &quot;Respondents with Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop ctrl_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF TREATED UNITS WITHOUT A CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION&#10;gen treat_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 0 &amp; treatment == 1&#10;egen tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 = total(treat_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Treated Individuals without Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop treat_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF CTRL UNITS WITHOUT A CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION&#10;gen ctrl_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 0 &amp; treatment == 0&#10;egen tot_ctrl_not_talk_by_section_1 = count(ctrl_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_ctrl_not_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Number of Control People Not Interact Often with Politician&quot;&#10;&#10;gen tot_study_infrequent_1 = tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 + tot_ctrl_not_talk_by_section_1&#10;label variable tot_study_infrequent_1 &quot;Respondents without Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop ctrl_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** As of now, the voting data includes unadjusted regressors, but not adjusted ones&#10;replace mun_id = c_mun_id&#10;save &quot;${gen_data_folder}/voting_data_unadjusted.dta&quot;, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>input.single_source.import_recode_save.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/120068/Prepare data.do:24-40; askrec_pol_private_2013 if year2013 == 1&#10;replace ask_public_stacked = ask_pol_public_2013 if year2013 == 1&#10;&#10;replace rainfall_std_stacked = rainfall_2013_std if year2013 == 1&#10;replace rainfall_std_stacked_og = rainfall_2013_std_og if year2013 == 1&#10;&#10;* Controls for citizen engagement&#10;replace mem_assoc = e_mem_assoc_comunitaria_ if year2013 == 1&#10;replace pres_assoc = e_ever_commun_president if year2013 == 1&#10;&#10;&#10;** Keep only observations for which we have data on interactions with politicians&#10;keep if frequent_interactor != .&#10;&#10;&#10;** Keep only variables needed for stacked regressions &#10;keep ask_private_stacked askrec_private_stacked ask_nowater_private_stacked ask_public_stacked ///&#10;mun_id c_mun_id year2012 year2013 b_clusters ///&#10;treatment rainfall_std_stacked rainfall_std_stacked_og frequent_interactor mem_assoc pres_assoc b_voted&#10;&#10;&#10;** Variable labels &#10;label var ask_private_stacked &quot;Asked politician for private good&quot;&#10;label var askrec_private_stacked &quot;Asked politician for private good and received it&quot;&#10;label var ask_nowater_private_stacked &quot;Asked politician for private good excluding water&quot;&#10;label var ask_public_stacked &quot;Asked politician for public good&quot;&#10;label var year2012 &quot;Year 2012 dummy&quot;&#10;label var year2013 &quot;Year 2013 dummy&quot;&#10;label var frequent_interactor &quot;Frequent interactions with politician outside campaign&quot;&#10;label var mem_assoc &quot;Household member is/was member of community association&quot;&#10;label var pres_assoc &quot;Household member is/was president of community association&quot;&#10;label var rainfall_std_stacked &quot;Rainfall shock (standardized)&quot;&#10;label var rainfall_std_stacked_og &quot;Rainfall shock&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;quietly compress&#10;save &quot;${final_data_folder}/clientelism_individual_data_stacked.dta&quot;, Main_Tables.do:584-676; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=18f2088cee3150ffb8d3e6f9c147a6560b814f4962101cee0e8832b3ec1ca78b; code_sha256=3ab245b1eca369643db4c33f92c5bd3d868dd478f718ba2bf60ec40cab04a88c</small><pre>use &quot;${final_data_folder}/clientelism_individual_data_stacked.dta&quot;, clear&#10;	&#10;&#10;*==========================================================================*&#10;**## Create and label treatment variables&#10;*==========================================================================*&#10;&#10;gen treat_freq = treatment * frequent_interactor&#10;gen rain_std_freq = rainfall_std_stacked * frequent_interactor&#10;&#10;&#10;** Label treatment variables&#10;label variable treatment &quot;$\beta_1$: Cisterns Treatment&quot;&#10;label variable treat_freq &quot;$\beta_2$: Cisterns Treatment $\times$ Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;label variable rainfall_std_stacked &quot;$\beta_3$: Rainfall Shock&quot;&#10;label variable rain_std_freq &quot;$\beta_4$: Rainfall Shock $\times$ Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;label variable frequent_interactor &quot;$\beta_5$: Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;*==============================================================================*&#10;**## (1) Heterogeneous Effects of Cisterns by Clientelist Relationship, Private Requests&#10;*==============================================================================*&#10;local var = &quot;ask_private_stacked&quot;&#10;&#10;eststo: reg `var&#x27; frequent_interactor treatment treat_freq i.mun_id year2012, cluster(b_clusters)&#10;estadd_obs&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 1&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y&quot;)&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 0&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y_control&quot;)&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 0 &amp; frequent_interactor == 1&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y_control_freq&quot;)&#10;&#10;&#10;** Effect of Cisterns Treatment for Individuals in Clientelist Relationship&#10;lincom treatment + treat_freq&#10;&#10;local beta: display %9.3fc r(estimate)&#10;local treat_frequent_b1 = strtrim(&quot;`beta&#x27;&quot;)&#10;&#10;local se: display %9.3fc r(se)&#10;local treat_frequent_se1 = &quot;(&quot; + strtrim(&quot;`se&#x27;&quot;) + &quot;)&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;*==============================================================================*&#10;**## (2) Heterogeneous Effects of Rainfall by Clientelist Relationship, Private Requests&#10;*==============================================================================*&#10;local var = &quot;ask_private_stacked&quot;&#10;&#10;eststo: reg `var&#x27; frequent_interactor rainfall_std_stacked rain_std_freq i.mun_id year2012, cluster(b_clusters)&#10;estadd_obs&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 1&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y&quot;)&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 0&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y_control&quot;)&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 0 &amp; frequent_interactor == 1&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y_control_freq&quot;)&#10;&#10;&#10;** Effect of Rainfall for Individuals in Clientelist Relationship&#10;lincom rainfall_std_stacked + rain_std_freq&#10;&#10;local beta: display %9.3fc r(estimate)&#10;local rain_frequent_b1 = strtrim(&quot;`beta&#x27;&quot;)&#10;&#10;local se: display %9.3fc r(se)&#10;local rain_frequent_se1 = &quot;(&quot; + strtrim(&quot;`se&#x27;&quot;) + &quot;)&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;*==============================================================================*&#10;**## (3) Heterogeneous Effects of Cisterns and Rainfall by Clientelist Relationship, Private Requests&#10;*==============================================================================*&#10;local var = &quot;ask_private_stacked&quot;&#10;&#10;eststo: reg `var&#x27; frequent_interactor treatment rainfall_std_stacked treat_freq rain_std_freq i.mun_id year2012, cluster(b_clusters)&#10;estadd_obs&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 1&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y&quot;)&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 0&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y_control&quot;)&#10;estadd_mean_Y, depvar(`var&#x27;) condition(&quot;if e(sample) == 1 &amp; treatment == 0 &amp; frequent_interactor == 1&quot;) scalar_name(&quot;mean_Y_control_freq&quot;)&#10;&#10;&#10;** Effect of Cisterns Treatment for Individuals in Clientelist Relationship&#10;lincom treatment + treat_freq&#10;&#10;local beta: display %9.3fc r(estimate)&#10;local treat_frequent_b2 = strtrim(&quot;`beta&#x27;&quot;)&#10;&#10;local se: display %9.3fc r(se)&#10;local treat_frequent_se2 = &quot;(&quot; + strtrim(&quot;`se&#x27;&quot;) + &quot;)&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;** Effect of Rainfall for Individuals in Clientelist Relationship&#10;lincom rainfall_std_stacked + rain_std_freq&#10;&#10;local beta: display %9.3fc r(estimate)&#10;local rain_frequent_b2 = strtrim(&quot;`beta&#x27;&quot;)&#10;&#10;local se: display %9.3fc r(se)&#10;local rain_frequent_se2 = &quot;(&quot; + strtrim(&quot;`se&#x27;&quot;) + &quot;)&quot;&#10;</pre></details> | <details><summary><code>dataset.finalize_section_adjacency_drop.v1 [exclude]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/173341/dofiles/3. Voting_Analysis_Cleaning.do:781-837; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=b81992d1b82969e738404e16d113d35611dd81bda7d2b8ce760533c595641f4d; code_sha256=814999db903f2185ee7dc8c665c4e56fb2045b715d362a39710d380193286fbf</small><pre>** Collapse data at the voting section level&#10;drop if location_id == .&#10;sort location_id c_voter_section_no_ &#10;drop if location_id == location_id[_n-1] &amp; c_voter_section_no_ == c_voter_section_no_[_n-1] &#10;&#10;&#10;*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*&#10;**## Final Treatment Variables &#10;*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*&#10;&#10;rename tot_treatment_by_section_1 tot_treat_by_section_1&#10;&#10;&#10;** Keep only variables used in final analysis&#10;keep c_mun_id mun_id c_voter_section_no_ location_id c_vote_here_ b_clusters ///&#10;name_match vp_match pty_match coalition_match eligible_in_2008 ///&#10;incumbent_votes_section challenger_votes_section turnout blank_null PT_votes pres_coalition_votes gov_coalition_votes right_leaning_votes ///&#10;tot_treat_by_section_2* tot_study_2* eligible tot_treat_by_section_1 tot_study_1 ///&#10;tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 tot_study_frequent_1 tot_study_infrequent_1 ///&#10;avg_age avg_school avg_gender missing_age missing_school missing_gender ///&#10;candidate_A_incumbent* candidate_B_incumbent* candidate_C_incumbent*&#10;&#10;&#10;** Variable labels&#10;label var c_voter_section_no_ &quot;Voter section number&quot;&#10;label var c_mun_id &quot;Municipal ID&quot;&#10;label var eligible &quot;No. of registered voters&quot;&#10;label var turnout &quot;Turnout&quot;&#10;label var eligible_in_2008 &quot;No. of registered voters in 2008&quot;&#10;&#10;foreach cand in &quot;A&quot; &quot;B&quot; &quot;C&quot; {&#10;	&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is incumbent mayor&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_vp &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is incumbent vice-mayor&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_pty &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is from incumbent party&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_coalition &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is from incumbent coalition&quot;&#10;}&#10;&#10;label var location_id &quot;Voting location ID&quot;&#10;label var incumbent_votes_section &quot;Votes for incumbent mayor&quot;&#10;label var challenger_votes_section &quot;Votes for challenger candidate&quot;&#10;label var blank_null &quot;Blank and null votes&quot;&#10;label var name_match &quot;Incumbent mayor was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var vp_match &quot;Incumbent vice-mayor was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var pty_match &quot;Candidate from incumbent party was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var coalition_match &quot;Candidate from incumbent coalition was cand A/B/C&quot;&#10;label var avg_age &quot;Respondent age (mean)&quot;&#10;label var missing_age &quot;Missing respondent age (mean, section)&quot;&#10;label var avg_school &quot;Respondent years of education (mean)&quot;&#10;label var missing_school &quot;Missing respondent years of education (mean, section)&quot;&#10;label var avg_gender &quot;Respondent is female (mean)&quot;&#10;label var missing_gender &quot;Missing respondent gender (mean, section)&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;** Save final electoral dataset for the overall sample&#10;quietly compress&#10;save &quot;${final_data_folder}/voting_data.dta&quot;, replace&#10;</pre></details> | — | support gap: <code>label.value_label_define_attach</code>, <code>label.value_label_extend_modify</code>, <code>label.variable_label_near_creation</code><br>code-template exact gap: <code>label.value_label_define_attach</code>, <code>label.value_label_extend_modify</code>, <code>label.variable_label_near_creation</code> |

if exporter==&quot;MMR&quot; &amp; year&lt;1989&#10;replace importer=&quot;BUR&quot; if importer==&quot;MMR&quot; &amp; year&lt;1989&#10;duplicates list year exporter importer&#10;*********************************&#10;*NOTE: EUN appears as duplicates*&#10;*********************************&#10;drop if exporter==&quot;EUN&quot; {&#10;&#10;	preserve&#10;	&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	duplicates drop exporter, force&#10;	gen randn = runiform()&#10;	egen rank=rank(randn), unique&#10;	keep if rank&gt;=46&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_exporter`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	rename exporter importer&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_importer`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	&#10;	restore&#10;&#10;}&#10;	&#10;* initialise matrices&#10;mat B_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat N_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;mat index = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat N_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat SE = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat loCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat hiCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;* open data set&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_large, clear&#10;&#10;* main estimation&#10;qui ppmlhdfe trade RTA_3, absorb(idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj brdr_time) cluster(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;mat B_temp = e(b)&#10;local B_main = B_temp[1,1]	&#10;local N_main = e(N)&#10;&#10;* run 1000 jackknife iterations&#10;forval jiter=1(1)`N_jiter&#x27; {&#10;&#10;	preserve&#10;	&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	duplicates drop exporter, force&#10;	gen randn = runiform()&#10;	egen rank=rank(randn), unique&#10;	keep if rank&gt;=46&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_exporter`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	rename exporter importer&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_importer`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	&#10;	restore&#10;&#10;}&#10;	&#10;* initialise matrices&#10;mat B_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat N_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;mat index = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat N_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat SE = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat loCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat hiCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;* open data set&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_large, clear&#10;&#10;* main estimation&#10;qui ppmlhdfe trade RTA_3, absorb(idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj brdr_time) cluster(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;mat B_temp = e(b)&#10;local B_main = B_temp[1,1]	&#10;local N_main = e(N)&#10;&#10;* run 1000 jackknife iterations&#10;forval jiter=1(1)`N_jiter&#x27; {&#10;&#10;	di &quot;`jiter&#x27;&quot;&#10;		&#10;	* open data set&#10;	use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_large, clear	&#10;&#10;	forval p=1(1)4 {&#10;&#10;		preserve&#10;		&#10;		capture drop _merge_id_ci&#10;		qui merge m:1 exporter using &quot;${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_exporter`jiter&#x27;.dta&quot;&#10;		rename _merge _merge_id_ci&#10;		capture drop _merge_id_cj&#10;		qui merge m:1 importer using &quot;${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_importer`jiter&#x27;.dta&quot;&#10;		rename _merge _merge_id_cj&#10;		&#10;		if `p&#x27;==1 {&#10;			qui keep if _merge_id_ci==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==2 {&#10;			qui drop if _merge_id_ci==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==3 {&#10;			qui keep if _merge_id_cj==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==4 {&#10;			qui drop if _merge_id_cj==3&#10;		}&#10;		&#10;		qui ppmlhdfe trade RTA_3, absorb(idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj brdr_time) cluster(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;		mat B_temp = e(b)&#10;		mat B_jack[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = B_temp[1,1]	&#10;		mat N_jack[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = e(N)&#10;		mat index[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = (`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;&#10;		&#10;		restore&#10;			&#10;	}&#10;</pre></details> | — | support gap: none |

!mi(lg_rgdpna_percap_1)&#10;		gen Cheibobxgdp_1 = Democ_dummy_1*lg_rgdpna_percap_1 if !mi(Democ_dummy_1) &amp; !mi(lg_rgdpna_percap_1)&#10;		gen W_BdMxgdp_1 = W_BdM_1*lg_rgdpna_percap_1 if !mi(W_BdM_1) &amp; !mi(lg_rgdpna_percap_1)&#10;		gen W_eprxgdp_1 = W_epr_1*lg_rgdpna_percap_1 if !mi(W_epr_1) &amp; !mi(lg_rgdpna_percap_1)&#10;		gen VDemxgdp_1 = v2x_polyarchy_1*lg_rgdpna_percap_1 if !mi(v2x_polyarchy_1) &amp; !mi(lg_rgdpna_percap_1)&#10;&#10;		*First generate lags&#10;		xtset cid t&#10;		clonevar Z_democracy_2=Z_democracy_1&#10;		replace Z_democracy_2=l.Z_democracy_1&#10;		clonevar Z_democracy_3=Z_democracy_2&#10;		replace Z_democracy_3=l.Z_democracy_2&#10;		clonevar Z_democracy_4=Z_democracy_3&#10;		replace Z_democracy_4=l.Z_democracy_3&#10;		clonevar Z_democracy_5=Z_democracy_4&#10;		replace Z_democracy_5=l.Z_democracy_4	&#10;		clonevar Z2_polity2_2 = Z2_polity2_1&#10;		replace Z2_polity2_2=l.Z2_polity2_1&#10;		clonevar Z2_cheibob_2 = Z2_cheibob_1&#10;		replace Z2_cheibob_2=l.Z2_cheibob_1&#10;		clonevar Z2_W_BdM_2 = Z2_W_BdM_1&#10;		replace Z2_W_BdM_2 = l.Z2_W_BdM_1&#10;		clonevar Z2_W_epr_2 = Z2_W_epr_1&#10;		replace Z2_W_epr_2 = l.Z2_W_epr_1&#10;		clonevar Z2_v2x_polyarchy_2 = Z2_v2x_polyarchy_1&#10;		replace Z2_v2x_polyarchy_2 = l.Z2_v2x_polyarchy_1&#10;&#10;		*Generate interaction term with multiple lags of Z&#10;		gen Z_democxgdp_2=Z_democracy_2*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z_democxgdp_3=Z_democracy_3*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z_democxgdp_4=Z_democracy_4*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z_democxgdp_5=Z_democracy_5*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z2_polity2xgdp_2=Z2_polity2_2*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z2_cheibobxgdp_2=Z2_cheibob_2*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z2_W_BdMxgdp_2=Z2_W_BdM_2*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z2_W_eprxgdp_2=Z2_W_epr_2*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;		gen Z2_VDemxgdp_2=Z2_v2x_polyarchy_2*lg_rgdpna_percap_1&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>panel.cohort_anticipation_exit_filter.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:177-227; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=3366f4a4b80e5943bee2feea8764f04f1d0caa3a04ecc1b1a51faaeb621d7008</small><pre>foreach dtaset in small medium large {&#10;&#10;	// load trade data&#10;	use ${usr_data}/data_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, clear&#10;	&#10;	* preliminary variable definitions&#10;	rename RTA_DGD RTA&#10;	egen id_ci = group(exporter)&#10;	egen id_cj = group(importer)&#10;&#10;	* set panel IDs&#10;	xtset id_ci_cj year, yearly&#10;&#10;	* determine treatment cohorts&#10;	egen first_treat=csgvar(RTA), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;	* shift RTA/WTO 3 years forward for anticipation effects&#10;	gen RTA_3 = F3.RTA&#10;	egen first_treat_3=csgvar(RTA_3), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;	gen WTO_3 = F3.WTO&#10;	&#10;	* define exclude dummy for three anticipation years&#10;	gen exclude = (first_treat-1==year &#124; first_treat-2==year &#124; first_treat-3==year)&#10;	&#10;	// drop always-treated units&#10;	drop if first_treat_3 == 1980&#10;&#10;	// drop exits&#10;	sort exporter importer year&#10;	gen D_RTA = 0&#10;	replace D_RTA = RTA-RTA[_n-1] if id_ci_cj==id_ci_cj[_n-1]&#10;	egen exit = min(D_RTA), by(id_ci_cj)&#10;	drop if exit==-1&#10;	drop exit D_RTA&#10;&#10;	// define 5-year interval&#10;	gen int_5yr = mod(year,5)&#10;	&#10;	// define border-time ID&#10;	gen brdr=1 if exporter!=importer&#10;	replace brdr=0 if brdr==.&#10;	egen brdr_time=group(brdr year)&#10;	&#10;	// define variables&#10;	gen lnDIST = ln(DIST)&#10;	gen lntrade = ln(trade)&#10;&#10;	// save data set&#10;	save ${usr_data}/data_sdid_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, replace&#10;&#10;}&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>panel.turnover_event_time.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_dataforregressions.do:160-180; source_kind=deposited_research_code_partial_reference; source_sha256=17bc94ae771e9981454402904e4fab5b010da6730319ed715f731f632cb62afa; code_sha256=e91ca20c1a26e4b95d35c873a5b01f2b7c880571fd0ba19e3d7a603877cc663b</small><pre>// A school is &quot;adp&quot; if there is an ADP principal by the end of the year	&#10;	by rbd: egen aux_min_adp = min(year) if adp == 1&#10;	egen min_adp=min(aux_min_adp), by(rbd)&#10;	drop aux_min_adp&#10;	&#10;	cap drop t&#10;	g t = year - min_adp &#10;	replace t = 0 if EverADP == 0&#10;	&#10;	&#10;// Change principal: for placebo exercise&#10;	xtset rbd year&#10;	g change_ppal = 1 if id_prin != l.id_prin&#10;	g YearPpalChange = year if change_ppal == 1 &amp; year != 2008&#10;	bys rbd: egen aux_MinYearPpalChange = min(YearPpalChange)&#10;	egen MinYearPpalChange = min(aux_MinYearPpalChange), by(rbd)&#10;	drop aux_MinYearPpalChange&#10;	g t_pla = year - MinYearPpalChange&#10;	&#10;	g PostChangePpal = 1 if (year &gt;= MinYearPpalChange)  &amp; MinYearPpalChange &lt;= 2011  &amp; MinYearPpalChange &gt;= 2010&#10;	replace PostChangePpal = 0 if PostChangePpal == .&#10;</pre></details> | — | — | support gap: <code>xtset_tsset.declared_delta_or_frequency</code>, <code>xtset_tsset.panel_id_time</code>, <code>xtset_tsset.single_time_series</code><br>code-template exact gap: <code>xtset_tsset.declared_delta_or_frequency</code>, <code>xtset_tsset.panel_id_time</code>, <code>xtset_tsset.single_time_series</code> |

!mi(lg_rgdpna_percap_1)&#10;		gen W_eprxgdp_1 = W_epr_1*lg_rgdpna_percap_1 if !mi(W_epr_1) &amp; !mi(lg_rgdpna_percap_1)&#10;		gen VDemxgdp_1 = v2x_polyarchy_1*lg_rgdpna_percap_1 if !mi(v2x_polyarchy_1) &amp; Add gravity variables*&#10;**************************&#10;joinby exporter importer year using ${tmp_data}\dgd_sdid, unmatched(master)&#10;tab _m&#10;********************************************************************************&#10;*NOTE: About 2.6% of the observations in the trade data are not matched in DGD.*&#10;*The reason is the coding of some countries in DGD, e.g., HKG is missing before* &#10;*1997. Given the small percent of mismatched observations, we should be fine.  *&#10;********************************************************************************&#10;drop _m&#10;******************************************************************************************&#10;*c. Make sure all gravity covariates, except distance, are set to zero for domestic trade*&#10;******************************************************************************************&#10;local gravvars &quot;CNTG LANG CLNY EU WTO RTA FTA&quot; &#10;foreach x of local gravvars{&#10;replace `x&#x27;=0 if exporter==importer&#10;}&#10;*************************&#10;*d. Check for duplicates*&#10;*************************&#10;duplicates list year exporter importer&#10;*************************************&#10;*e. Create some additional variables*&#10;*************************************	&#10;egen idt_ci = group(exporter year)&#10;egen idt_cj = group(importer year)&#10;egen id_ci_cj = group(exporter importer)&#10;compress&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_large, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>sample.trade_share_threshold.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:135-151; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=fba0a33bea533832ae9cb0f0e4b82c00c127b52036c1558586b3c406e4072f58</small><pre>use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;keep if exporter!=importer&#10;collapse (sum) trade, by(exporter)&#10;sort trade&#10;gen sum_trade_cum=sum(trade)&#10;egen sum_trade_all=sum(trade)&#10;gen ratio= sum_trade_cum/sum_trade_all&#10;drop if ratio&lt;0.01&#10;keep exporter&#10;save ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta, replace&#10;rename exporter importer&#10;save ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta, replace&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;joinby importer using ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta&#10;joinby exporter using ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta&#10;keep year exporter importer trade CLNY CNTG LANG DIST RTA_DGD WTO_DGD gdp_pwt_cur_d gdp_pwt_cur_o idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_medium.dta, replace&#10;</pre></details><br><details><summary><code>sample.trade_share_threshold_small.v1 [reference_only]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:153-171; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=65d25ded06a2515cfcf05e86f6cc3a5620e55443783cef69525dc5e18c68bd00</small><pre>*g. Create small sample*&#10;**************************&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;keep if exporter!=importer&#10;collapse (sum) trade, by(exporter)&#10;sort trade&#10;gen sum_trade_cum=sum(trade)&#10;egen sum_trade_all=sum(trade)&#10;gen ratio= sum_trade_cum/sum_trade_all&#10;drop if ratio&lt;0.02&#10;keep exporter&#10;save ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta, replace&#10;rename exporter importer&#10;save ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta, replace&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_large.dta, clear&#10;joinby importer using ${tmp_data}\imp_small.dta&#10;joinby exporter using ${tmp_data}\exp_small.dta&#10;keep year exporter importer trade CLNY CNTG LANG DIST RTA_DGD WTO_DGD gdp_pwt_cur_d gdp_pwt_cur_o idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_small.dta, replace&#10;</pre></details> | — | <details><summary><code>corpus-209030-021207 [occurrence_support]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/209030/(0) STATA - Build Dataset/Build Dataset.do:331; - <details><summary><code>clean.standardize_on_fixed_esample.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_dataforregressions.do:386-393; source_kind=deposited_research_code_partial_reference; source_sha256=17bc94ae771e9981454402904e4fab5b010da6730319ed715f731f632cb62afa; code_sha256=7db2605fac93772cf09299af61a2a17f66edc307d4432f25aa2450e387d397ec</small><pre>	foreach var in  simce_4to simce_8vo simce_2do NEM {&#10;		cap drop Std_`var&#x27;&#10;		gen Std_`var&#x27; = .&#10;		cap sum `var&#x27; if sample2==1&#10;		cap replace Std_`var&#x27;=(`var&#x27;-`r(mean)&#x27;)/`r(sd)&#x27; if sample2==1&#10;	}&#10;	cap drop SimceIndex&#10;	egen SimceIndex = rowmean(Std_simce_4to Std_simce_8vo Std_simce_2do)&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>clean.treatment_full_vs_control.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/173341/dofiles/1. Cleaning.do:388-402; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=ba31bd7e4709d0e83f3976f25c7eef5883d962799d09fc29596761c6c2fba7ce; code_sha256=19f66acb1ee62bc03dd253bed91f184ae804e21021d893742a0c1e1306ad54b6</small><pre>gen TREATMENT = .&#10;replace TREATMENT = 0 if b_cl_assignment == 3 									// no cistern&#10;replace TREATMENT = 1 if b_cl_assignment == 2 &amp; b_cistern_assignment == 0 		// no cistern, partial treatment&#10;replace TREATMENT = 2 if b_cl_assignment == 2 &amp; b_cistern_assignment == 1 		// cistern, partial treatment&#10;replace TREATMENT = 3 if b_cl_assignment == 1  									// cistern, full treatment&#10;		&#10;		&#10;* Define treatment variable as 1 if an individual is in a full-treatment cluster and 0 if an individual is in a control cluster. &#10;gen treatment = 1 if TREATMENT == 3&#10;replace treatment = 0 if TREATMENT == 0&#10;label var treatment &quot;Cisterns Treatment&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;* Drop unneeded variables &#10;drop TREATMENT b_cl_assignment b_cistern_assignment&#10;</pre></details>

is incumbent vice-mayor&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_pty &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; is from incumbent party&quot;&#10;	label var candidate_`cand&#x27;_incumbent_coalition &quot;Candidate `cand&#x27; Voting_Analysis_Cleaning.do:621-675; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=b81992d1b82969e738404e16d113d35611dd81bda7d2b8ce760533c595641f4d; code_sha256=2492ed0d806a4f98fa2c682e3ebe28ff28afec4584ef61016365b882fe93d80b</small><pre>** Counting number of treated people by section number&#10;egen tot_treatment_by_section_1 = total(treatment), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_treatment_by_section_1 &quot;Treated Individuals&quot;&#10;&#10;gen tot_study_temp = 1 if treatment == 0 &#124; treatment == 1 &#10;egen tot_study_1 = total(tot_study_temp) if c_voter_section_no_ != ., by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_study_1 &quot;Respondents&quot;&#10;&#10;gen tot_control_by_section_1 = tot_study_1 - tot_treatment_by_section_1&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF TREATED UNITS WITH CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION	&#10;gen treat_frequent = treatment * frequent_interactor&#10;label var treat_frequent &quot;Treatment $\times$ Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;						&#10;gen treat_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 1 &amp; treatment == 1&#10;egen tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 = total(treat_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Treated Individuals with Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop treat_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF CTRL UNITS WITH CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION &#10;gen ctrl_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 1 &amp; treatment == 0&#10;egen tot_ctrl_talk_by_section_1 = total(ctrl_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_ctrl_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Number of Control People Interact Often with Politician&quot;&#10;&#10;gen tot_study_frequent_1 = tot_treat_talk_by_section_1 + tot_ctrl_talk_by_section_1&#10;label variable tot_study_frequent_1 &quot;Respondents with Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop ctrl_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF TREATED UNITS WITHOUT A CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION&#10;gen treat_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 0 &amp; treatment == 1&#10;egen tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 = total(treat_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Treated Individuals without Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop treat_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** COUNTING NUMBER OF CTRL UNITS WITHOUT A CLIENTELIST RELATIONSHIP IN A GIVEN SECTION&#10;gen ctrl_talk_temp = 1 if frequent_interactor == 0 &amp; treatment == 0&#10;egen tot_ctrl_not_talk_by_section_1 = count(ctrl_talk_temp), by(c_mun_id c_voter_section_no_)&#10;label variable tot_ctrl_not_talk_by_section_1 &quot;Number of Control People Not Interact Often with Politician&quot;&#10;&#10;gen tot_study_infrequent_1 = tot_treat_not_talk_by_section_1 + tot_ctrl_not_talk_by_section_1&#10;label variable tot_study_infrequent_1 &quot;Respondents without Clientelist Relationship&quot;&#10;&#10;drop ctrl_talk_temp&#10;&#10;&#10;** As of now, the voting data includes unadjusted regressors, but not adjusted ones&#10;replace mun_id = c_mun_id&#10;save &quot;${gen_data_folder}/voting_data_unadjusted.dta&quot;, replace&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>figure.coefplot_named_estimates_labels.v1 [candidate]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/175582/Code/Analysis/Figures_MainText/Figures_MainText.do:548-559; source_kind=deposited_research_code_source_pattern; source_sha256=23b3e7875eefd46bc9fa0deb17b66841cba3e3db8fdbea47f3600d71b1492577; code_sha256=528312de14602e90d609800a478cd934c04feda4c6db4a426c8ad0df3fef58fe</small><pre>coefplot (reg_std_q44e, keep(T_*) mcolor(navy) ciopts(lcolor(navy))) ///&#10;		(reg_std_q44k, keep(T_*) mcolor(navy) ciopts(lcolor(navy))) /// &#10;		(reg_std_q44m , keep(T_*) mcolor(navy) ciopts(lcolor(navy))) /// &#10;		(reg_std_q44w , keep(T_*) mcolor(navy) ciopts(lcolor(navy))) /// &#10;		(reg_std_q44x , keep(T_*) mcolor(navy) ciopts(lcolor(navy))) /// &#10;		(reg_eq_index_ds, keep(T_*) mcolor(navy) ciopts(lcolor(navy))), ///&#10;		bgcolor(white) plotregion(color(white)) graphregion(color(white)) ///&#10;		xline(0, lwidth(thin)) nooffsets grid(w) ///&#10;		xtitle(&quot;Treatment effect&quot; &quot;(standard deviations)&quot;) legend(off) ///&#10;		coeflabels(&quot;T_std_q44k&quot;=`&quot; &quot;Academic perform depression/anxiety/&quot; &quot;seasonal affect disorder&quot; &quot;&#x27;, ///&#10;			labsize(normal)) ///	&#10;		xscale(r(-0.15 0.15)) xtick(-0.15(0.05)0.15) xlabel(-0.15 -0.10 -0.05 0 0.05 0.10 0.15, labsize(normal))&#10;</pre></details>

- <details><summary><code>figure.event_study_csdid_event_plot.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/175582/Code/ado/e/five_estimators_example.do:57-64; source_kind=deposited_research_code_source_pattern; source_sha256=9092710a972900367d449c2f06c7916f4e22f1a4ff6ae2e9a171333c85921ec1; code_sha256=b3fc67120f3ff6d510f32d79f61deeba15996c59c6d3e04045770edb5c902f42</small><pre>// Estimation with cldid of Callaway and Sant&#x27;Anna (2020)&#10;gen gvar = cond(Ei==., 0, Ei) // group variable as required for the csdid command&#10;timer on 3&#10;csdid Y, ivar(i) time(t) gvar(gvar) notyet&#10;estat event, estore(cs) // this produces and stores the estimates at the same time&#10;timer off 3&#10;event_plot cs, default_look graph_opt(xtitle(&quot;Periods since the event&quot;) ytitle(&quot;Average causal effect&quot;) xlabel(-9(1)4) ///&#10;	title(&quot;Callaway and Sant&#x27;Anna (2020)&quot;)) stub_lag(E#) stub_lead(E_#) together&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>figure.event_study_did_imputation_event_plot.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/175582/Code/ado/e/five_estimators_example.do:39-44; source_kind=deposited_research_code_source_pattern; source_sha256=9092710a972900367d449c2f06c7916f4e22f1a4ff6ae2e9a171333c85921ec1; code_sha256=3a0965dc99bd9ba225b953567febd279a66ab2442ec37e3aa0e4599e131aca6a</small><pre>// Estimation with did_imputation of Borusyak et al. (2021)&#10;timer on 1&#10;did_imputation Y i t Ei, allhorizons pretrend(5)&#10;timer off 1&#10;event_plot, default_look graph_opt(xtitle(&quot;Periods since the event&quot;) ytitle(&quot;Average causal effect&quot;) ///&#10;	title(&quot;Borusyak et al. (2021) imputation estimator&quot;) xlabel(-3(1)4))&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>figure.event_study_did_multiplegt_event_plot.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/175582/Code/ado/e/five_estimators_example.do:48-52; source_kind=deposited_research_code_source_pattern; source_sha256=9092710a972900367d449c2f06c7916f4e22f1a4ff6ae2e9a171333c85921ec1; code_sha256=8eedb5e57d3b716993ba00f30ea1b89f243249a2fd47991f376b2297850362c7</small><pre>// Estimation with did_multiplegt of de Chaisemartin and D&#x27;Haultfoeuille (2020)&#10;timer on 2&#10;did_multiplegt Y i t D, robust_dynamic dynamic(5) placebo(5) breps(100) cluster(i) &#10;timer off 2&#10;event_plot e(estimates)#e(variances), default_look graph_opt(xtitle(&quot;Periods since the event&quot;) ytitle(&quot;Average causal effect&quot;) title(&quot;de Chaisemartin and D&#x27;Haultfoeuille (2020)&quot;) xlabel(-4(1)4)) stub_lag(Effect_#) stub_lead(Placebo_#) together&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>figure.event_study_row_order_parmest.v1 [excluded]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_recruitment_figures.do:15-64; source_kind=deposited_research_code_partial_reference; source_sha256=b5b389d5573ff48c2c307026e6c1d118a64e4f5df08baba1ecf57659e89e5e1d; code_sha256=54ba5add69bade76d453fefaf0b8bb6b4f79028bfd4d937dad35392bf8b51863</small><pre>	g d_4_t = EverADP * t == -4&#10;	g d_3_t = EverADP * t == -3&#10;	g d_2_t = EverADP * t == -2&#10;	g d_1_t = EverADP * t == -1&#10;	g d0_t = EverADP * t == 0&#10;	g d1_t = EverADP * t == 1&#10;	g d2_t = EverADP * t == 2&#10;	g d3_t = EverADP * t == 3&#10;&#10;	global T &quot;d_4_t d_3_t d_2_t d0_t d1_t d2_t d3_t&quot;&#10;	&#10;	* Panel A: No controls&#10;		reghdfe SDall_fe_prin_2wayfe $T if t&gt;=-4 &amp; t &lt;= 3 &amp; year &gt;= 2009 &amp; year &lt;= 2016 &amp; sample == 1 , a(year rbd t) vce(cluster rbd)&#10;		test d_4_t = d_3_t = d_2_t = 0&#10;		preserve&#10;				estimate store coef &#10;							&#10;			* Create data set of reg coefficients and confidence intervals &#10;				parmest, norestore&#10;&#10;			* Keep parameters of interest&#10;				drop if _n &gt; 7		&#10;				rename t tstat&#10;&#10;			* Time variable from and since event&#10;	&#10;				g       t = _n - 5 if _n &lt;= 3&#10;				replace t = _n - 4 if _n &gt; 3								&#10;								&#10;			* Add coefficient in t == -1&#10;				local tot = 1 + _N&#10;				set obs `tot&#x27;&#10;				replace t = -1 if _n == `tot&#x27;&#10;				replace estimate = 0 if estimate == .&#10;				replace min95 = 0 if min95 == .&#10;				replace max95 = 0 if max95 == .&#10;				sort t&#10;							&#10;			* Figure&#10;				twoway (scatter estimate t, mc(navy)) (rcap min95 max95 t, lc(gs12) fc(gs12)), ///&#10;					yline(0, lc(black) ) xline(-0.5, lc(navy) lw(medium) ) ///&#10;					graphregion(color(white)) ///&#10;					ylabel(-0.05(0.05)0.15, format(%9.2f)) /// &#10;					xlabel(-4(2)3) xtitle(&quot;Years since ADP&quot;) ///					&#10;					graphregion(color(white)) /// &#10;					scale(1.3) ///&#10;					legend(off)&#10;&#10;			* Export 			&#10;			graph export &quot;Figures/Figure_4_A.pdf&quot;, replace&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>figure.event_study_sun_abraham_event_plot.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/175582/Code/ado/e/five_estimators_example.do:66-80; source_kind=deposited_research_code_source_pattern; source_sha256=9092710a972900367d449c2f06c7916f4e22f1a4ff6ae2e9a171333c85921ec1; code_sha256=58699ab849c5f7117846cda69438993397533160c67e61b937b1391c215b906e</small><pre>// Estimation with eventstudyinteract of Sun and Abraham (2020)&#10;sum Ei&#10;gen lastcohort = Ei==r(max) // dummy for the latest- or never-treated cohort&#10;forvalues l = 0/5 {&#10;	gen L`l&#x27;event = K==`l&#x27;&#10;}&#10;forvalues l = 1/14 {&#10;	gen F`l&#x27;event = K==-`l&#x27;&#10;}&#10;drop F1event // normalize K=-1 (and also K=-15) to zero&#10;timer on 4&#10;eventstudyinteract Y L*event F*event, vce(cluster i) absorb(i t) cohort(Ei) control_cohort(lastcohort)&#10;timer off 4&#10;event_plot e(b_iw)#e(V_iw), default_look graph_opt(xtitle(&quot;Periods since the event&quot;) ytitle(&quot;Average causal effect&quot;) xlabel(-9(1)4) ///&#10;	title(&quot;Sun and Abraham (2020)&quot;)) stub_lag(L#event) stub_lead(F#event) together&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>figure.event_study_twfe_event_plot.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/175582/Code/ado/e/five_estimators_example.do:85-90; source_kind=deposited_research_code_source_pattern; source_sha256=9092710a972900367d449c2f06c7916f4e22f1a4ff6ae2e9a171333c85921ec1; code_sha256=c4709d76274ac2d77460b55fe13fd53d0e38e98f7f5cd32747e559cbf33c6d3b</small><pre>// TWFE OLS estimation (which is correct here because of treatment effect homogeneity). Some groups could be binned.&#10;timer on 5&#10;reghdfe Y F*event L*event, a(i t) cluster(i)&#10;timer off 5&#10;event_plot, default_look stub_lag(L#event) stub_lead(F#event) together graph_opt(xtitle(&quot;Days since the event&quot;) ytitle(&quot;OLS coefficients&quot;) xlabel(-4(1)4) ///&#10;	title(&quot;OLS&quot;))&#10;</pre></details>

- <details><summary><code>merge.teacher_year_attrition_flow.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/148522/A.6.TableA6_Teacher_Attrition.do:54-89; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=f19cc8a831d0e8289296a3844124d95b32d2df21cf15ac98114a42de381972ac; code_sha256=bd13ccfa9a8b57994baa74eb8320fbceec076c1aeeda4f044bfeacf289931386</small><pre>* Collapses data to one observation per teacher and year&#10;collapse (max) teacher_gain_y1 teacher_loss_y1 teacher_any_y1 taught_gr* ///&#10;(mean) math_std mathI_std value_added M_value_added, by(teacher_id sy)&#10;&#10;tempfile working2&#10;save `working2&#x27;&#10;&#10;clear	&#10;use &quot;$data/long_term_testscores.dta&quot;&#10;&#10;gen teacher_any_y1 = 0&#10;replace teacher_any_y1 = 1 if teacher_gain_y1==1 &#124; teacher_loss_y1==1&#10;&#10;collapse (max) teacher_gain_y1 teacher_loss_y1 teacher_any_y1 ///&#10;(mean) mathI_std value_added M_value_added, by(teacher_id sy)&#10;&#10;append using `working2&#x27;&#10;&#10;* Create indicators for teachers showing up in our sample in a given year&#10;levelsof sy, local(levels) &#10;foreach l of local levels {&#10; gen I_`l&#x27; = 0&#10; replace I_`l&#x27; = 1 if sy == `l&#x27;&#10; bys teacher_id: egen in_sample_`l&#x27; = max(I_`l&#x27;)&#10; drop I_`l&#x27;&#10; }&#10;&#10;keep if in_sample_201011 == 1&#10;&#10;collapse (max) teacher_gain_y1 teacher_loss_y1 teacher_any_y1 taught_gr* ///&#10;(mean) math_std mathI_std value_added M_value_added in_sample_*, by(teacher_id)&#10;&#10;* Creates outcome for pooled data&#10;gen times_in_sample = in_sample_201112 + in_sample_201213 + in_sample_201314 ///&#10;+ in_sample_201415 + in_sample_201516&#10;gen frac_in_sample = times_in_sample / 5&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>outlier.winsorize_two_sided_aggregate_wage.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_dataforregressions.do:50-59; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=17bc94ae771e9981454402904e4fab5b010da6730319ed715f731f632cb62afa; code_sha256=53fe2a056ef5bb57e11a8b824752eaa0be97a3343e2c65ce6b43ed5f642a1d72</small><pre>	preserve&#10;		use &quot;Data/clean/Panel_Wages.dta&quot;, clear&#10;		gen N=1&#10;		collapse (sum) All N, by(rbd)&#10;		gen WageSchool=All/N&#10;		winsor2 WageSchool, c(1 99) replace&#10;		keep rbd WageSchool&#10;		tempfile Wage&#10;		save `Wage&#x27;, replace&#10;	restore&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>panel.cohort_anticipation_exit_filter.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/10_data_preparation.do:177-227; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=28d3bf8bde49cce093ad583a3f62a9089981154fdb71f35892d39a8d9c42cc38; code_sha256=3366f4a4b80e5943bee2feea8764f04f1d0caa3a04ecc1b1a51faaeb621d7008</small><pre>foreach dtaset in small medium large {&#10;&#10;	// load trade data&#10;	use ${usr_data}/data_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, clear&#10;	&#10;	* preliminary variable definitions&#10;	rename RTA_DGD RTA&#10;	egen id_ci = group(exporter)&#10;	egen id_cj = group(importer)&#10;&#10;	* set panel IDs&#10;	xtset id_ci_cj year, yearly&#10;&#10;	* determine treatment cohorts&#10;	egen first_treat=csgvar(RTA), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;	* shift RTA/WTO 3 years forward for anticipation effects&#10;	gen RTA_3 = F3.RTA&#10;	egen first_treat_3=csgvar(RTA_3), tvar(year) ivar(id_ci_cj)&#10;	gen WTO_3 = F3.WTO&#10;	&#10;	* define exclude dummy for three anticipation years&#10;	gen exclude = (first_treat-1==year &#124; first_treat-2==year &#124; first_treat-3==year)&#10;	&#10;	// drop always-treated units&#10;	drop if first_treat_3 == 1980&#10;&#10;	// drop exits&#10;	sort exporter importer year&#10;	gen D_RTA = 0&#10;	replace D_RTA = RTA-RTA[_n-1] if id_ci_cj==id_ci_cj[_n-1]&#10;	egen exit = min(D_RTA), by(id_ci_cj)&#10;	drop if exit==-1&#10;	drop exit D_RTA&#10;&#10;	// define 5-year interval&#10;	gen int_5yr = mod(year,5)&#10;	&#10;	// define border-time ID&#10;	gen brdr=1 if exporter!=importer&#10;	replace brdr=0 if brdr==.&#10;	egen brdr_time=group(brdr year)&#10;	&#10;	// define variables&#10;	gen lnDIST = ln(DIST)&#10;	gen lntrade = ln(trade)&#10;&#10;	// save data set&#10;	save ${usr_data}/data_sdid_`dtaset&#x27;.dta, replace&#10;&#10;}&#10;</pre></details>

source_sha256=5c103077af301876c7297c30830457b9c5b72c0e28f006a6ba5596c57ba9a871; code_sha256=842fcdc9fe72d67aef804d86965ad658fa9088b355a48c38f95285cbbd3a188a</small><pre>	*create a long dataset by year&#10;	keep spending* gp_code_lgd21 shrid closestpop_1000 pc91_pca_tot_p pc01_pca_tot_p ///&#10;		pc11_pca_tot_p gp_code_jj11_num gp_code_lgd21_num pc11_gp_jj11 pc11_gp_lgd21&#10;	&#10;	reshape long spending_sfc4_ spending_asv_ spending_brgf_ spending_nfc12_ spending_nfc13_ ///&#10;		spending_nfc14_ spending_sfc2_ spending_sfc3_ spending_mdm_ spending_own_ spending_sn_ ///&#10;			spending_ssa_ spending_sbm_ spending_vn_, ///&#10;		i(gp_code_lgd21 shrid closestpop_1000 pc91_pca_tot_p pc01_pca_tot_p pc11_pca_tot_p ///&#10;			gp_code_jj11_num gp_code_lgd21_num pc11_gp_jj11 pc11_gp_lgd21) ///&#10;		j(year)&#10;		&#10;	&#10;	gen spending_sfc_ = spending_sfc2_ + spending_sfc3_ + spending_sfc4_&#10;	&#10;	gen spending_nfc_ = spending_nfc12_ + spending_nfc13_ + spending_nfc14_&#10;	drop spending_nfc12_ spending_nfc13_ spending_nfc14_&#10;	&#10;	gen spending_small_ = spending_asv_ + spending_brgf_ + spending_mdm_ + spending_sn_ + spending_ssa_ + spending_sbm_ + spending_vn_&#10;	&#10;	foreach var of varlist spending_* {&#10;	&#10;		gen `var&#x27;pc_ = cond( year &lt; 2015, `var&#x27; / pc11_gp_jj11, `var&#x27; / pc11_gp_lgd21)&#10;		replace `var&#x27; = `var&#x27;/1000&#10;		&#10;	}&#10;	&#10;	egen spending_total_ = rowtotal( spending_sfc_ spending_nfc_ spending_small_)&#10;		replace spending_total_ = . if missing(spending_sfc_pc)&#10;	&#10;	&#10;	egen spending_total_pc_ = rowtotal(*_pc_)&#10;		replace spending_total_pc_ = . if missing(spending_sfc_pc)&#10;	&#10;	&#10;	gen village_pop_running = cond( year &lt;= 2015, pc91_pca_tot_p, pc11_pca_tot_p)&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>panel.turnover_event_time.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/191751/script_dataforregressions.do:160-180; source_kind=deposited_research_code_partial_reference; source_sha256=17bc94ae771e9981454402904e4fab5b010da6730319ed715f731f632cb62afa; code_sha256=e91ca20c1a26e4b95d35c873a5b01f2b7c880571fd0ba19e3d7a603877cc663b</small><pre>// A school is &quot;adp&quot; if there is an ADP principal by the end of the year	&#10;	by rbd: egen aux_min_adp = min(year) if adp == 1&#10;	egen min_adp=min(aux_min_adp), by(rbd)&#10;	drop aux_min_adp&#10;	&#10;	cap drop t&#10;	g t = year - min_adp &#10;	replace t = 0 if EverADP == 0&#10;	&#10;	&#10;// Change principal: for placebo exercise&#10;	xtset rbd year&#10;	g change_ppal = 1 if id_prin != l.id_prin&#10;	g YearPpalChange = year if change_ppal == 1 &amp; year != 2008&#10;	bys rbd: egen aux_MinYearPpalChange = min(YearPpalChange)&#10;	egen MinYearPpalChange = min(aux_MinYearPpalChange), by(rbd)&#10;	drop aux_MinYearPpalChange&#10;	g t_pla = year - MinYearPpalChange&#10;	&#10;	g PostChangePpal = 1 if (year &gt;= MinYearPpalChange)  &amp; MinYearPpalChange &lt;= 2011  &amp; MinYearPpalChange &gt;= 2010&#10;	replace PostChangePpal = 0 if PostChangePpal == .&#10;</pre></details>

- <details><summary><code>postest.estimator_equality_unchecked_merge.v1 [excluded]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/30_Table_1.do:79-106; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=7e9a373530546e93ca57889d7f78be059142fdc6ab511adbd51cf4b22888d30f; code_sha256=17704590bcbe0906fe388efd9ee84862d8a742b0d587bca69ba17a0909fe2829</small><pre>* generate separate dataset for TWFE&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_small, clear&#10;merge 1:1 idt_ci idt_cj using ${usr_data}\sample_JWDID_`spec&#x27;&#10;keep if sample_JWDID_`spec&#x27;==1&#10;drop sample_JWDID_`spec&#x27; _merge&#10;gen ETWFE=0&#10;replace first_treat_3=0&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_sdid_small_ETWFE0, replace&#10;&#10;* generate separate dataset for ETWFE&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_small, clear&#10;merge 1:1 idt_ci idt_cj using ${usr_data}\sample_JWDID_`spec&#x27;&#10;keep if sample_JWDID_`spec&#x27;==1&#10;drop sample_JWDID_`spec&#x27; _merge&#10;gen ETWFE=1&#10;replace RTA_3=0&#10;save ${usr_data}\data_sdid_small_ETWFE1, replace&#10;&#10;* run SUR&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_small_ETWFE0, clear&#10;append using ${usr_data}\data_sdid_small_ETWFE1&#10;jwdidstacked trade, ivar(id_ci_cj) tvar(year) gvar(first_treat_3) method(ppmlhdfe) fe(idt_ci#ETWFE idt_cj#ETWFE brdr_time#ETWFE year#ETWFE id_ci_cj#ETWFE) exovar(i1.RTA_3#c.__tr__ ETWFE)&#10;margins  ,  subpop(if __etr__==1) at(__tr__=(0 1)) over(ETWFE) noestimcheck contrast(atcontrast(r)) predict(xb) post&#10;test _b[r2vs1._at@0.ETWFE]=_b[r2vs1._at@1.ETWFE]&#10;local p_value: display %4.3f `r(p)&#x27;&#10;estimates restore JWDID_`spec&#x27;&#10;estadd scalar p_test = `p_value&#x27;&#10;estimates store JWDID_`spec&#x27;&#10;</pre></details>

- <details><summary><code>robustness.heterogeneity_nlcom_grid.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/120068/Analyse data.do:183-201; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=e41a8bd865b5954c0d8fddee81caa53ca524860e6331bde698044a136cc43b52; code_sha256=c2d9ee94dd01aea85768476df4822957fe9680d83c945629644e04e4cf72fafa</small><pre>				matrix M=J(9,4,.)&#10;				matrix coln M = x coeff ll95 ul95&#10;				matrix rown M = 5 6 7 8 9 10 11 12 13&#10;				local n 1&#10;			forvalues x=5(1)13 {&#10;				nlcom _b[acemoglu_democ_1] + _b[democxgdp_1]*(`x&#x27;)&#10;					local tvalue= invttail(e(df_m),0.025)&#10;					matrix b = r(b)&#10;					local bw = b[1,1]&#10;					matrix v = r(V)&#10;					local vw = v[1,1]&#10;					local se = sqrt(`vw&#x27;)&#10;					local lbx1 = `bw&#x27; - `tvalue&#x27;*`se&#x27;&#10;					local ubx1 = `bw&#x27; + `tvalue&#x27;*`se&#x27; &#10;					cap matrix M[`n&#x27;,1]= `x&#x27;, `bw&#x27;, `lbx1&#x27; , `ubx1&#x27;&#10;					local replace&#10;					local append append&#10;					local n `n&#x27;+1&#10;			}&#10;</pre></details>

Folders&#10;*------------------------------------------------------------------------------*&#10;&#10;** Data Folders&#10;global source_data_folder &quot;${master_file_path}/data/source_data&quot;&#10;global gen_data_folder &quot;${master_file_path}/data/generated_data&quot;&#10;global final_data_folder &quot;${master_file_path}/data/final_data&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;** Code Folders&#10;global main_code_folder &quot;${master_file_path}/dofiles&quot;&#10;&#10;&#10;** Output Folders&#10;global main_tables_folder &quot;${master_file_path}/output/main_tables&quot;&#10;global appendix_tables_folder &quot;${master_file_path}/output/appendix_tables&quot;&#10;global figures_folder &quot;${master_file_path}/output/figures&quot;&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>simulation.jackknife_nested_loops.v1 [excluded]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/194561/code/30_Table_1f_TWFE_jackknife_large.do:24-105; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=fda660e578ca0dd12843c68dc7bc91431b155b22e18b9d48755087074a7a9cef; code_sha256=f3a5e104ef417a33ca7281b13dad04f52d4e75da5aaa741f2e105dfce284f415</small><pre>forval i=1(1)`N_jiter&#x27; {&#10;&#10;	preserve&#10;	&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	duplicates drop exporter, force&#10;	gen randn = runiform()&#10;	egen rank=rank(randn), unique&#10;	keep if rank&gt;=46&#10;	keep exporter&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_exporter`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	rename exporter importer&#10;	qui save ${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_importer`i&#x27;, replace&#10;	&#10;	restore&#10;&#10;}&#10;	&#10;* initialise matrices&#10;mat B_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat N_main = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;mat index = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat N_jack = J(`N_iter&#x27;,1,.)&#10;mat B = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat SE = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat loCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;mat hiCI = J(1,1,.)&#10;&#10;* open data set&#10;use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_large, clear&#10;&#10;* main estimation&#10;qui ppmlhdfe trade RTA_3, absorb(idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj brdr_time) cluster(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;mat B_temp = e(b)&#10;local B_main = B_temp[1,1]	&#10;local N_main = e(N)&#10;&#10;* run 1000 jackknife iterations&#10;forval jiter=1(1)`N_jiter&#x27; {&#10;&#10;	di &quot;`jiter&#x27;&quot;&#10;		&#10;	* open data set&#10;	use ${usr_data}\data_sdid_large, clear	&#10;&#10;	forval p=1(1)4 {&#10;&#10;		preserve&#10;		&#10;		capture drop _merge_id_ci&#10;		qui merge m:1 exporter using &quot;${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_exporter`jiter&#x27;.dta&quot;&#10;		rename _merge _merge_id_ci&#10;		capture drop _merge_id_cj&#10;		qui merge m:1 importer using &quot;${usr_data}\Simulation\data_sdid_large_jackknife_importer`jiter&#x27;.dta&quot;&#10;		rename _merge _merge_id_cj&#10;		&#10;		if `p&#x27;==1 {&#10;			qui keep if _merge_id_ci==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==2 {&#10;			qui drop if _merge_id_ci==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==3 {&#10;			qui keep if _merge_id_cj==3&#10;		}&#10;		if `p&#x27;==4 {&#10;			qui drop if _merge_id_cj==3&#10;		}&#10;		&#10;		qui ppmlhdfe trade RTA_3, absorb(idt_ci idt_cj id_ci_cj brdr_time) cluster(id_ci_cj)&#10;&#10;		mat B_temp = e(b)&#10;		mat B_jack[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = B_temp[1,1]	&#10;		mat N_jack[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = e(N)&#10;		mat index[(`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;,1] = (`jiter&#x27;-1)*`N_p&#x27;+`p&#x27;&#10;		&#10;		restore&#10;			&#10;	}&#10;</pre></details>
- <details><summary><code>treatment.teacher_any_arm_mapping.v1 [reference]</code></summary><small>source=corpus/packages/148522/6.Table6_LongTermTeacher.do:12-15; source_kind=deposited_research_code; source_sha256=c63a303ba246e7aa45fe9cb0e71286e06b30cd27b4db949b4efacc561c7a5fd8; code_sha256=d909aafd232f970c9b6928df20029cbdbb63c686d7a2f21e857a3e4004f5f01e</small><pre>gen teacher_any_y1 = 0&#10;replace teacher_any_y1 = 1 if teacher_gain_y1==1 &#124; teacher_loss_y1==1&#10; &#10;keep if treat_desc_y1~=&quot;Not In Study&quot;&#10;</pre></details>
